From August 30 to September 3, 2026, Chinese President Xi Jinping visited Kyrgyzstan for the Shanghai Cooperation Organization \(SCO\) summit and conducted state visits to Kyrgyzstan and Egypt, as reported by Wang Yi on September 4. The visit centered on reforming global governance through the &\#x27;Shanghai Spirit,&\#x27; resulting in an updated SCO Charter, the launch of new cooperation centers for port economy and basic education, and commitments to 100 new scientific projects over three years. Bilaterally, China and Kyrgyzstan signed a treaty on permanent good-neighborly friendship alongside 30 cooperation documents, while China and Egypt agreed to advance a community with a shared future, signing similar agreements covering artificial intelligence, trade, and cultural exchange.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** The Shanghai Cooperation Organization, founded 25 years ago, has expanded to 27 member states across Asia, Europe, and Africa, representing nearly half the world&\#x27;s population. This tour followed the previous year&\#x27;s Tianjin summit, which established strategic blueprints for the next decade that are now being implemented through various economic and technological platforms.

**「Impact」** The agreements legally solidify China&\#x27;s strategic partnerships in Central Asia and the Middle East while establishing new multilateral frameworks for digital, green, and infrastructure cooperation. These outcomes provide concrete mechanisms for regional security dialogue and economic integration among member states.

**「Impact」** The increased leadership role of researchers under 45 in national funding programs indicates a structural shift in China&\#x27;s scientific ecosystem, potentially accelerating R&amp;D velocity in critical hardware and AI sectors. On September 1, 2026, the Zhishenxing-1 liquid oxygen-kerosene launch vehicle from Chizhou, Anhui, successfully reached orbit, marking the province&\#x27;s first locally manufactured rocket of its kind. Reported by People&\#x27;s Daily on September 4, the article highlights how Anhui is expanding new-quality productive forces by upgrading traditional industries and cultivating strategic emerging and future sectors. Key technical milestones include quantum technology, nuclear fusion energy, brain-computer interfaces, and commercial aerospace, with high-tech manufacturing value-added growing 44.6% year-on-year in the first half of 2026. rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** Anhui has positioned itself as a national hub for advanced manufacturing and scientific research, anchored by institutions like the Deep Space Exploration Laboratory and Hefei&\#x27;s Comprehensive National Science Center. The province has been actively implementing policy directives issued since 2024 to develop futuristic industries such as brain-computer interfaces and commercial spaceflight through coordinated urban clusters and public testing platforms.

**「Impact」** Anhui-based firms and research institutions will gain access to expanded commercial space and biomedical technology markets, with targeted industrial growth potentially creating thousands of high-skilled jobs in quantum, fusion, and aerospace sectors. Uncertainty remains around whether commercial satellite and rocket production timelines will align with the 2028 targets given supply chain and funding constraints.

<a id="item-tech-news-88"></a>
### [China Post integrates e-commerce, logistics, and finance to support rural revitalization](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30179037.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Published on September 4, 2026, in People&\#x27;s Daily, this report details China Post&\#x27;s strategy to aggregate commercial, logistics, capital, and information flows to address rural revitalization challenges. During the 14th Five-Year Plan period, China Post’s agricultural-related loans grew by over 1 trillion yuan, while online sales reached 73.2 billion yuan and agricultural parcel deliveries exceeded 22.1 billion items. The company leverages its unique status as the only central enterprise with postal offices in every township and mail service in every village to operate the Youle.com e-commerce platform, which drove over 54.5 million packaging orders through &quot;one product, one policy&quot; operations in the first half of 2026. Additionally, Postal Savings Bank of China maintained an agricultural loan balance of 2.51 trillion yuan by end-2025, utilizing mobile &quot;backpack banking&quot; to serve rural clients lacking traditional collateral.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** China Post operates the most extensive rural distribution network in China, comprising approximately 48,000 village-level service stations and 85,000 rural delivery routes. It employs a &quot;self-operated + agency&quot; model that places roughly 70% of its financial outlets in county-level and rural areas, allowing it to bridge the gap between remote agricultural producers and national markets.

**「Impact」** The integration of these services creates a self-reinforcing cycle where successful agricultural sales generate parcel volume for logistics and customer inflow for financial services. This model provides a scalable framework for rural economic development, with goals to exceed 100 billion yuan in rural e-commerce transaction volume and 15 billion parcels annually by the end of the 15th Five-Year Plan.

A Sept. 4, 2026 People&\#x27;s Daily article cites a Party archive record of a political assessment given to Xi Zhongxun during a Northwest Bureau high-level cadre conference. The record describes office visits from local residents as the usual daily scene and portrays Xi as naturally approachable, ready to pause his work for ordinary people without bureaucratic distance. Interpreting this as a historical example, the essay links those practices to the current mandate for officials to treat serving the public as their primary achievement and warns that economic growth does not automatically deepen ties with the masses.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** Xi Zhongxun was a senior Chinese Communist Party leader known for promoting pragmatic, mass-line approaches to local governance. The People&\#x27;s Daily commentary follows a longer emphasis within the Party on correcting performance-oriented politics and maintaining close relations with ordinary residents.

**「Impact」** The article targets party officials and grassroots cadres in China, urging them to prioritize direct public service and avoid distance from ordinary residents. It frames the archival record as a practical standard for evaluating political performance rather than a technical policy change.

The article argues that China&\#x27;s poverty alleviation success and民生 strategy succeed by combining immediate action with long-term capacity building, avoiding both rushed outcomes and ungrounded promises. rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

It references China&\#x27;s socialist初级阶段 context, where development imbalances require policies that match existing conditions without overreaching.

**「Impact」** This editorial reinforces official policy rhetoric around measured民生 development, likely influencing local government approaches to service expansion and infrastructure planning. <a id="item-tech-news-113"></a>
### [China&\#x27;s high-tech manufacturing profits surge amid industrial upgrade](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30179070.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Data released by China&\#x27;s National Bureau of Statistics shows that from January to July 2026, profit in above-average-sized high-tech manufacturing enterprises grew by 50.1% year-on-year, pulling total industrial profit growth by 9.6 percentage points. The figures reflect continued advancement up the manufacturing value chain and rapid expansion of new growth drivers during the first seven months of the year. National bureau data described overall industrial production as steadily expanding with a positive profit trend for firms above the designated size.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** China has been steering industrial policy toward high-tech and advanced manufacturing sectors to reduce reliance on low-margin export assembly and bolster technological self-reliance. Profit growth in the high-tech manufacturing segment is commonly used as a gauge of whether that structural upgrade is accelerating across the broader industrial base.

**「Impact」** The reported high-tech profit surge signals stronger earnings momentum for firms in advanced manufacturing and suggests policy support for industrial upgrading is contributing to improved corporate performance in those sectors. Uncertainty remains about whether the gain is concentrated in specific subindustries or reflects broad-based improvement across regions and ownership types.

<a id="item-tech-news-131"></a>
### [Expert commentary on recognizing practice outcomes in graduate degree evaluation](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30179088.html) ⭐️ ?/10

A 4 September 2026 People&\#x27;s Daily expert commentary argues that evaluating graduate students through practice outcomes is a constructive reform that breaks reliance on the single standard of academic theses. The article distinguishes research-oriented disciplines, where thesis writing remains essential, from applied and professional fields such as medicine, engineering machinery, and publishing, where practice-aligned outputs better match disciplinary attributes. It frames this shift not as lowering graduation standards, but as requiring higher实践能力 benchmarks tied to real industry needs, while noting that traditional theses still offer irreplaceable theoretical value. The piece calls for continued refinement of practice-outcome recognition criteria and quality oversight to cultivate more application-oriented talents who serve industrial development.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** Chinese higher-education reform has increasingly emphasized the separation of academic and professional degree tracks, seeking evaluation methods that align with distinct career-oriented learning outcomes. Practice-outcome assessment typically involves industry projects, design portfolios, clinical cases, or technical reports rather than conventional literature-based dissertations.

**「Impact」** The commentary signals editorial support for universities expanding practice-based graduation options in applied master&\#x27;s and professional programs, which may accelerate policy adoption in fields such as medicine and engineering. Whether institutions can implement consistent recognition standards and oversight remains contingent on further regulatory guidance.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-132"></a>
### [伽师县新梅产业带动二十余万人就业](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30179089.html) ⭐️ ?/10

In Jiashi County, Xinjiang, the prunus \(new plum/ximei\) industry has become a major economic driver, with planted area stable at 570,000 mu accounting for 50% of China&\#x27;s total and 2026 output expected to reach over 410,000 tonnes, about 70% of national production. To overcome the fruit&\#x27;s short shelf life of three to five days, the county built a 500,000-tonne cold-chain storage system, extended the sales window from 10–20 days to two to six months, and paired it with rural e-commerce stations and express delivery coverage that now reaches 100% of villages. Technical teams provide standardized orchard management, while digital supervision and full-chain traceability have earned the product green-food and climate-optimized certifications. The integrated planting, packaging, processing, and sales chain now employs more than 200,000 people, pushes per-capita farmer income to 17,000 yuan, and has generated over ten billion yuan in industry output.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** 新梅，又称西梅，是新疆伽师县的特产品种。伽师县地处戈壁滩区域，过去主要种植玉米和棉花，农业效益较低。近年来，通过引入标准化种植技术、冷链物流体系和电商销售网络，当地成功将新梅产业打造为乡村振兴的重要支柱。

**「Impact」** Jiashi County farmers growing prunes now earn over 5,000 yuan per mu compared with less than 1,000 yuan previously, and the county&\#x27;s cold-chain and e-commerce infrastructure enables nationwide fresh delivery within 48–72 hours. The model demonstrates how targeted agri-tech extension and logistics investment can transform a highly perishable regional crop into a scalable national commodity.

### [Yan&\#x27;an Road Bridge in Bengbu Achieves Main Span Closure](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30179091.html) ⭐️ ?/10

On September 3, 2026, the main bridge of the Yan&\#x27;an Road Bridge in Bengbu, Anhui province, was successfully closed after the precise hoisting and placement of its closure section steel box girder. As the largest span cable-stayed bridge crossing the Huai River basin, the project marks a critical milestone toward full opening to traffic. Upon completion, the bridge will significantly alleviate local transportation pressure and eliminate the need for residents to take detours to cross the river.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** Bridge closure refers to the final stage of construction where the last segment of a bridge span is installed to connect both sides, creating a continuous structural system. Cable-stayed bridges like Yan&\#x27;an Road Bridge use tall towers and direct cables to support the deck, making them suitable for crossing wide waterways such as the Huai River.

**「Impact」** The closure enables continued construction toward full通车, which will directly benefit Bengbu commuters by reducing travel time and improving regional connectivity across the Huai River.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-135"></a>
### [2026 China Culture and Tourism Industry Expo Opens in Tianjin](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30179092.html) ⭐️ ?/10

The 2026 China Culture and Tourism Industry Expo opened in Tianjin on September 3, hosted by the Ministry of Culture and Tourism. Under the theme &quot;Deepening Culture-Tourism Integration and Intelligent Creation of a Better Life,&quot; the four-day event covers 100,000 square meters and features 2,540 exhibiting enterprises from 30 Chinese provinces, regions, and cities, as well as 20 countries and regions worldwide. The expo is organized into ten specialized zones, including digital new formats, cultural tourism equipment, and international cooperation, while simultaneously hosting 18 professional activities focused on regional collaboration, industrial innovation, and technology empowerment. Key initiatives include B2B matchmaking sessions, a dedicated service mini-program for precise supply-demand对接, and a comprehensive service desk offering one-stop support.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Context」** This national-level expo serves as a platform integrating成果展示 \(achievement display\), industry exchange, project matchmaking, and consumer experience. It reflects China&\#x27;s ongoing strategy to merge cultural and tourism industries while leveraging digital technologies to drive economic growth and international cooperation in the sector.

**「Impact」** The expo provides a major国家级 \(national-level\) platform for cultural and tourism enterprises to showcase innovations, establish business partnerships, and access international markets. Its emphasis on digital integration and B2B matchmaking directly supports industry modernization and cross-regional cooperation within China&\#x27;s growing cultural tourism sector.

<a id="item-tech-news-153"></a>
### [Sichuan Wangcang scales up retired solar panel recycling](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30179110.html) ⭐️ ?/10

As of September 2026, Yong&\#x27;an Guangxun Environmental Technology in Wangcang Economic Development Zone, Sichuan, processes retired photovoltaic modules on automated lines that discharge seven recyclable material streams in under a minute. The facility handles about 1,700 panels and 50 tonnes daily, achieving over 96% comprehensive material recovery and more than 99% organic film removal, with output valued at roughly 5,000 yuan per tonne. Aluminum frames, glass, copper, tin, silver, and silicon are routed to smelters and processors in Yunnan, Sichuan, Chongqing, Hunan, Jiangxi, and Hebei. The park was introduced through a East–West cooperation partnership with Hangzhou&\#x27;s Xiaoshan District in December 2023, with a total investment of 310 million yuan; Phase I is operational and Phase II plans expanded downstream refining capacity.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** Photovoltaic modules contain layered glass, polymer encapsulant, aluminum framing, copper/silver interconnects, and silicon cells, requiring separation to recover valuable materials and avoid landfill. China has been accelerating end-of-life solar infrastructure management as early installations reach retirement age, treating recovered resources as part of circular-economy industrial policy.

**「Impact」** The Wangcang operation provides a verified domestic reference for high-yield, automated PV module recycling that can be replicated by other western Chinese provinces with large installed bases. If Phase II completes its integrated refining lines, the project could expand recoverable silver and copper throughput beyond current outbound processing arrangements.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-155"></a>
### [Standards and certification shape China&\#x27;s rising companion medical service industry](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30178989.html) ⭐️ ?/10

The People&\#x27;s Daily reported on September 4, 2026 that medical companion services in China are expanding as an employment option for people ranging from fresh graduates to career changers. The article noted a newly issued national standard, the Socialized Medical Accompaniment Service Basic Requirements, which aims to clarify service boundaries and set a framework covering providers, supplies, platforms, personnel, content, processes, and evaluation. In practice, companions help patients navigate hospital logistics, prepare symptoms and documents, and coordinate examinations, while being cautioned not to offer medical diagnoses because they lack clinical qualifications. Industry growth is also being shaped by local certification efforts, such as Chengdu’s special vocational ability assessment that has issued certificates to hundreds of trainees, alongside crackdowns on ticket scalpers who risk confusing legitimate services with illegal resales.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** This article discusses the emerging profession of medical companions \(陪诊师\) in China, particularly in cities like Chengdu and Hangzhou. It highlights the growing demand driven by an aging population, increased mobility, and the complexity of modern healthcare systems.

**「Impact」** The standard and local certification programs are expected to raise baseline service quality and give patients clearer recourse, while platforms and trainers face continued pressure to screen providers and manage disputes. Patients and families should still verify credentials and service terms because the broader industry lacks a unified national occupational license and practitioner quality remains uneven.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-156"></a>
### [Expert review on standardizing China&\#x27;s medical escort industry](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30178990.html) ⭐️ ?/10

A September 4, 2026 People&\#x27;s Daily expert commentary argues that China&\#x27;s growing medical escort industry needs clearer regulation to protect patients and ensure service quality. The article outlines core professional standards, including mastering clinical workflows and emergency basics, adhering to a &quot;three no&\#x27;s&quot; principle that prohibits making medical decisions, leaking privacy, or accepting kickbacks, and maintaining traceable service records. It recommends that hospitals implement准入备案 \(access registration\), behavior control, and public supervision mechanisms, while government bodies should enforce the national standard GB/T for social accompanied medical services and establish multi-department joint enforcement against price fraud and unlicensed operations.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** Medical escorts, or 陪诊师, are non-clinical assistants who help patients navigate hospital registration, queuing, and appointment scheduling, particularly serving elderly, disabled, or unfamiliar urban populations. In China,陪诊服务 has emerged as an informal sector without uniform national certification, prompting calls for alignment with existing health-service vocational standards and the《社会化陪同就医服务 基本要求》national standard.

**「Impact」** If adopted, the proposed hospital备案 and multi-agency enforcement mechanisms would formally recognize qualified escorts while restricting unregistered practitioners in clinical areas. The recommendation to integrate陪诊 into health-service vocational等级认定 could create pathway to certified employment for nurses, elder-care workers, and social workers, but compliance costs may initially limit small agencies&\#x27; ability to operate legally.

**「Community Discussion」** No community comments were available for this article, so there is no recorded public debate or practical experience data to summarize.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-157"></a>
### [China reports five-year decline in student myopia rates](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30178991.html) ⭐️ ?/10

National disease control officials reported on September 3, 2026, that the overall myopia rate among Chinese students fell to 49.9% in 2025, marking a cumulative 3.7 percentage point drop from 53.6% in 2018 and continuing a five-year downward trend. The National Disease Control and Prevention Administration emphasized preventive guidelines for the back-to-school season, advising students to engage in at least 70 minutes of daily moderate-to-vigorous physical activity and limit recreational screen time to under one hour per day. Officials also highlighted rising risks for common school传染病 such as influenza, norovirus, and hand-foot-and-mouth disease, recommending seasonal flu vaccination ideally completed in September or October.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** Myopia \(nearsightedness\) has been a persistent public health concern in China, with previous surveys indicating that over half of students were affected. The National Disease Control and Prevention Administration routinely issues seasonal health guidance around the start of the academic year to address disease outbreaks in densely populated school environments.

**「Impact」** Schools and families must implement stricter limits on screen time and ensure daily physical activity targets are met to sustain the declining myopia trend. Healthcare providers should prioritize flu vaccinations for children and elderly high-risk groups before the November peak season.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-158"></a>
### [China&\#x27;s Healthcare Security Bureau Releases DRG/DIP 3.0 Grouping Scheme](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30178992.html) ⭐️ ?/10

On September 3, 2026, China&\#x27;s National Healthcare Security Administration released the 3.0 version of the grouping scheme for Diagnosis-Related Groups \(DRG\) and Big Data Diagnosis-Intervention Packet \(DIP\) payments. The new scheme requires regions implementing DRG to complete transition preparations by the end of 2026, with full application by the end of March 2027. The DRG component includes 492 core groups and 825 subgroups, while the DIP component reduces its core disease database from 9,520 items in version 2.0 to 5,125 diseases, increasing case coverage from 92% to 95%. The policy explicitly prohibits medical institutions from using payment standards as spending limits for department or staff performance assessments.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** DRG and DIP are payment reform mechanisms used by China&\#x27;s healthcare system to control medical costs and improve efficiency by grouping diagnoses and treatments into standardized categories with fixed payment values. These systems replaced fee-for-service models to incentivize hospitals to provide cost-effective care while maintaining quality standards.

**「Impact」** Medical institutions in China will need to restructure their coding and billing systems to comply with the 3.0 version requirements by the specified deadlines, affecting how they manage patient discharge records and claim reimbursements. Hospitals that previously used DRG/DIP payment standards as internal performance metrics will need to redesign their incentive structures to comply with the new prohibition on linking payment standards to departmental assessments.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-159"></a>
### [Second National Women&\#x27;s Domestic Service Skills Competition Draws 18,000+](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30178993.html) ⭐️ ?/10

The 2026 Second National Women&\#x27;s Domestic Service Vocational Skills Competition opened on September 3 in Xi&\#x27;an, Shaanxi province, drawing over 18,000 participants nationwide with approximately 200 finalists competing in four events: maternity nursing, tidying and organizing, housekeeping, and family caregiving. Co-hosted by the All-China Women&\#x27;s Federation, the Ministry of Human Resources and Social Security, the National Development and Reform Commission, the Ministry of Commerce, and the All-China Federation of Trade Unions, the competition aims to cultivate high-quality female domestic service talent under the theme &\#x27;Set Domestic Service Benchmarks, Showcase Women&\#x27;s Strength.&\#x27; Compared with the previous edition, finalists show notable demographic shifts, with an average age of 36 years and a Bachelor&\#x27;s degree or above representation rising from 51.6% to 61%.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** National vocational skills competitions in China serve as key mechanisms for standardizing industry qualifications and linking competitive performance to official vocational skill level promotions. The domestic service sector has faced longstanding labor shortages, prompting government efforts to professionalize household care work and attract younger, better-educated entrants through policy incentives and public recognition.

**「Impact」** Finishing among the top performers in this competition can directly advance contestants&\#x27; recognized vocational skill levels and improve their employment and earning prospects within China&\#x27;s domestic service sector. The marked increase in educational attainment among finalists signals a trend toward greater professionalization, likely influencing both recruitment standards and public perception of household care work.

### [Foshan &\#x27;West League&\#x27; football tournament becomes Greater Bay Area cultural and economic engine](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/04/content_30179005.html) ⭐️ ?/10

The 20th edition of Foshan&\#x27;s amateur &quot;West League&quot; \(西甲\) football tournament concluded on August 23, 2026, at Yunxiushan Stadium with Tanwan Gaming defeating Baishui Huaxing 4-2. The 45-day competition featured 48 teams from 11 cities across the Greater Bay Area, including 125 international players from over 10 countries. This grassroots tournament, rooted in the region&\#x27;s manufacturing workforce since 2002, generated approximately 2.5 billion yuan in tourism and consumption through its ticket-driven economy, engaging 2 million visitors.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 4, 00:26

**「Background」** The Foshan &quot;West League&quot; originated as a football tournament for industrial workers in Sanshui District in 2002, inspired by the Spanish La Liga. It has evolved from casual matches on vacant land into a major regional sporting event with professional-grade facilities, leveraging Foshan&\#x27;s position as China&\#x27;s largest manufacturing hub with over 3 million industrial workers.

**「Impact」** The tournament has transformed local sports infrastructure, increasing Sanshui District&\#x27;s football facilities to 159 venues by 2025 \(1.8 per 10,000 residents\), making it a winter training base for 70 professional clubs. The event created a sustainable model for grassroots sports driving regional economic development through tourism and local business engagement.

By 2026, the Aichi-Nagoya program expanded to six individual golds: men&\#x27;s and women&\#x27;s lead, men&\#x27;s and women&\#x27;s bouldering, and two mixed team speed relays, reflecting the sport&\#x27;s growing specialization. China&\#x27;s rapid rise stems from focused investment in speed infrastructure and motion-capture analytics after early Olympic breakthroughs highlighted the event&\#x27;s global competitiveness.

**「Impact」** The team&\#x27;s detailed roster selections and medal targets will shape China&\#x27;s expected presence in six climbing events and reinforce the nation&\#x27;s status as a leading speed climbing power in Asia. ### [Gloria Steinem, feminist icon, dies at 92](https://www.nytimes.com/2026/09/03/us/politics/gloria-steinem-dead.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Gloria Steinem, widely regarded as a personification of the American feminist movement, has died at the age of 92, according to a New York Times report by Katharine Q. Seelye published on September 3, 2026. Steinem spent her career challenging entrenched sexist assumptions and advocating for women&\#x27;s equality in the workplace, the home, and broader society. Her activism and writing were instrumental in helping women develop a stronger sense of self-worth and achieve greater social and professional parity. The news marks the end of an era for a figure who helped define second-wave feminism and continued to influence gender discourse well into the 21st century.

rss · 纽约时报\(The New York Times\) · Sep 3, 19:35

**「Background」** Gloria Steinem emerged as a leading voice in the women&\#x27;s liberation movement during the 1960s and 1970s, co-founding Ms. magazine and organizing key advocacy efforts for equal rights. Her approach combined personal narrative with political analysis, making feminist theory accessible to a broad audience and inspiring generations of activists. Steinem remained a public figure and commentator on gender equality throughout her long life, adapting her message to evolving cultural and political landscapes.

**「Impact」** Steinem&\#x27;s death removes a central symbolic and strategic leader from the ongoing struggle for gender equality, likely prompting widespread reflection on the progress made and the work still needed. Her legacy continues to influence feminist organizations, media representations of women, and policy debates around workplace and domestic equity.

A federal judge has blocked President Trump&\#x27;s August executive order that sought to limit birthright citizenship. The ruling states that the issue was already settled by the Supreme Court, noting that &\#x27;no Presidential Executive Order can undo what the Supreme Court has done.&\#x27; This latest attempt follows previous efforts to reinterpret the 14th Amendment&\#x27;s Citizenship Clause regarding children born to undocumented immigrants in the United States.

rss · 华盛顿邮报\(The Washington Post\) · Sep 3, 13:49

**「Background」** The 14th Amendment guarantees citizenship to &\#x27;all persons born or naturalized in the United States,&\#x27; which has been interpreted since the 1898 Supreme Court case United States v. Wong Kim Ark to include children born to non-citizen parents. Courts have consistently rejected executive attempts to redefine birthright citizenship through executive orders.

**「Impact」** The ruling preserves birthright citizenship for children born in the U.S. to non-citizen parents and reinforces that constitutional interpretations by the Supreme Court cannot be overridden by executive action.

The Trump administration is proposing a rule that would strip private schools and colleges of their tax-exempt status if they provide targeted assistance to students based on race. This policy shift directly targets Diversity, Equity, and Inclusion \(DEI\) programs that offer race-conscious support. The move represents a significant escalation in federal efforts to restrict racial considerations in higher education. It could force many private institutions to either abandon such programs or face substantial financial penalties due to the loss of tax exemptions.

rss · ABC新闻\(American Broadcasting Company\) · Sep 3, 16:59

**「Background」** Tax-exempt status allows private nonprofit educational institutions to avoid federal income taxes, a benefit they receive in exchange for serving a public purpose. The proposed rule targets Diversity, Equity, and Inclusion \(DEI\) programs that provide targeted assistance based on race, potentially revoking tax exemption for institutions maintaining such practices.

**「Impact」** Private colleges with active DEI programs face potential loss of tax-exempt status, which could significantly increase their operational costs and reduce resources available for student aid. Institutions may respond by scaling back race-targeted support services or restructuring admissions and financial aid practices to comply with the proposed rule.

<a id="item-tech-news-268"></a>
### [Judge allows cull of 2,000 Catalina Island deer to protect native plants](https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/sep/03/catalina-island-deer-california) ⭐️ ?/10

A judge has authorized the Catalina Island Conservancy to kill nearly all of the island&\#x27;s approximately 2,000 mule deer to protect native plant species. The deer have been overgrazing native shrubs, which has allowed fast-igniting non-native grasses to spread and increase wildfire risk. The decision faces fierce opposition from locals and tourists who have long enjoyed the deer&\#x27;s presence around downtown Avalon.

rss · 卫报\(The Guardian\) · Sep 3, 23:19

**「Background」** Catalina Island, off the coast of Southern California, has had an introduced population of mule deer for nearly a century. Without natural predators, the herd grew large enough to significantly alter the island&\#x27;s ecosystem by overgrazing vegetation.

**「Impact」** The ruling will likely eliminate the visible deer population that has become a major tourist attraction on Catalina Island. It sets a precedent for using lethal culling as a primary tool for invasive herbivore management in protected island ecosystems.

Gloria Steinem, the prominent American feminist activist, has died at age 92. In a 2022 Spiegel interview, she outlined a comprehensive vision for societal improvement that addressed economic exploitation, gender inequality, racism, militarism, and environmental destruction. **「Why Now」** This is a notable cultural moment marking the passing of a central figure in modern feminist history.
